## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route: three sector talks, each with the same sequence of presenter slide, summary slide, then sub-business pages. Every content page has a numbered section badge, a banded takeaway headline and a sector color (rose, blue-teal, gold), so a reader can find their place. Many pages are very dense with small text, as on slides 4, 5, 25 and 29, and several have rough craft: the untranslated Japanese label and the ROIC row colliding with the footer on slide 3, and low-contrast white-on-pink banner headlines on slides 3 and 9. The density suits a circulated investor document better than a spoken presentation, and the consistent frame and the strongest pages (slides 9 and 24) keep it competent and usable, with consequential weaknesses.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the repeated page frame with its numbered badge, banded takeaway headline and sector color, and the clear single-idea layouts of slide 9 (reform table beside the ROIC bubble path) and slide 24 (before-and-after battery pack with photos).

**Most useful repair:** Fix the legibility and fit defects: raise the contrast of the pale-pink banner headlines on slides 3 and 9, translate the Japanese label on slide 3, and move the ROIC row on slide 3 off the footer.

**What would change the score:** Split the densest pages (slides 4, 5, 25, 29, 30) into a lead message with one visual each, and replace the empty divider pages 15, 16, 18 and 20 and the sparse closing page 36 with an agenda that carries a takeaway.

_Confidence medium. 37 of 37 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This Business Strategy Presentation from Mitsui Chemicals, dated December 17, 2025, is a set of three business-sector strategy talks given by division presidents. It opens with a title slide listing the three sectors: Life & Healthcare Solutions, Mobility Solutions and ICT Solutions. Each sector follows the same sequence: a presenter introduction, a summary slide tying strategy to FY28 and FY30 targets, then detailed sections on sub-businesses. The Life & Healthcare section covers vision care, agrochemicals, oral care and testing/diagnosis. The Mobility section covers elastomers, composite materials and solutions and new business. The ICT section covers semiconductor and assembly, imaging, converting and EUV pellicles. The deck closes with a slide stating a vision to be a global solutions company and a standard disclaimer on forward-looking statements. Slides worth studying include the FY19 to FY30 strategy timeline on slide 3, which combines a bar chart with an annotated history of investments, and the Kulzer restructuring waterfall on slide 9, which pairs a bubble chart of ROIC against invested capital.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Mitsui Chemicals Business Strategy Presentation dated December 17, 2025 have?**

The deck has 37 pages in total. Slide 1 is the title slide, and it lists the three sector businesses: Life & Healthcare Solutions, Mobility Solutions and ICT Solutions.

**What does the Mitsui Chemicals deck say about the global market growth for vision care lenses?**

Slide 4 gives the global market CAGR for 2024 to 2028 as 3% for ophthalmic lenses and 6% for high-refractive-index lenses. It lists MCI Vision Care at 6%+α, including peripheral domains.

**What is the FY30 target for the Mobility Solutions business at Mitsui Chemicals?**

Slide 17 shows the FY30 Target as 93B yen with an ROIC of 15%. Slide 19 also shows the FY30 Target at 93 in its operating income and EBITDA chart.

**What does the Mitsui Chemicals deck say about the growth of the genetic testing sector?**

Slide 12 states that the genetic testing sector has a CAGR of 8%, while the overall testing and diagnosis market has a CAGR of 5% for FY19 to FY25. It also notes that growth is being driven by a rise in infectious disease and personalized cancer treatments.

### Slide 3

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Places a yearly ROIC and profit trend chart alongside a dated list of acquisitions and partnerships, so the history and the forward targets read on one page.
- Review: Many pages are very dense with small text, as on slides 4, 5, 25 and 29, and several have rough craft: the untranslated Japanese label and the ROIC row colliding with the footer on slide 3, and low-contrast white-on-pink banner headlines on slides 3 and 9.
- Suggested repair: Fix the legibility and fit defects: raise the contrast of the pale-pink banner headlines on slides 3 and 9, translate the Japanese label on slide 3, and move the ROIC row on slide 3 off the footer.

**Layout**

Header band across the top with a blue numbered tab (1) at the left, a grey-pink section icon, the title block left of centre and the Mitsui Chemicals logo with a dark grey date box at top right. Below it a full-width pink headline band, then three columns of rounded boxes (Strategy to date, Environmental changes, Strategy for FY28 targets) with arrows between them, followed by a bar chart with category labels at left and FY axis labels below; copyright line sits bottom left and there is no visible slide number beyond the tab.

**Visuals**

Bar chart (column chart) titled 主な投資・施策 with ROIC values beneath each bar. Eight bars at left and right groups separated by a gap: FY19 14.8 (ROIC 5%), FY20 23.1 (9%), FY21 24.9 (8%), FY22 29.2 (7%), FY23 30.0 (6%), FY24 34.1 (7%), FY25 Outlook 35.5 (no ROIC printed), FY28 Target 67.0 (10%), FY30 Target 86.0 (12%). Values are printed inside the top of each bar in white bold. Bar fill #DCB1B1 (dusty rose); the FY25 Outlook bar is the same colour. No printed axis, units or ranges; the bar values are the only scale. A translucent pale pink arrow (#E3A7AB at 42% opacity) rises from behind the FY25 bar toward the FY28 bar, labelled +31.5B yen in grey. Logo: Mitsui Chemicals wing mark in blue and grey with the wordmark in dark grey. Section icon: a white heart-and-person pictogram on a rose square. Tab graphic: a navy #0B2A7A chevron with the number 1 in white.

### Slide 4

**Review notes**

- Review: Many pages are very dense with small text, as on slides 4, 5, 25 and 29, and several have rough craft: the untranslated Japanese label and the ROIC row colliding with the footer on slide 3, and low-contrast white-on-pink banner headlines on slides 3 and 9.
- What would change the score: Split the densest pages (slides 4, 5, 25, 29, 30) into a lead message with one visual each, and replace the empty divider pages 15, 16, 18 and 20 and the sparse closing page 36 with an agenda that carries a takeaway.

**Layout**

Full-width header band with a navy section chevron showing the number 2 at left, a rose icon, the title Lifecare Solutions : Vision Care in bold black at top left, the Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right with a dark grey date box beneath it. A full-width pale rose banner carries the two-line centred headline. Below, the left column holds a grey panel (value chain and CAGR box) over three stacked red-accented rows, and the right column holds the bulleted growth text above a stacked column chart with logos and a blue arrow at lower right. Margins are about 1 to 2 percent; the copyright line sits bottom left; no page number is shown.

**Visuals**

1. Value chain diagram in the grey panel: five rounded pill boxes in a row (Lens manufacturers, Lens processing laboratories, Retail & distribution, Consumers) joined by a grey (#A6A6A6) band with arrowhead pointing to Consumers. Under the first two pills are captions Lens polymerization and Prescription lens processing, cleaning, coating. Below them are three pills (Lens materials in #BE6E6E, Coating materials and Processing equipment in #DCB1B1 with blue #1A76BF outlines) with pale rose upward block arrows above them. Captions list Hard coat, Photochromic, Anti-fog, Hydrophobic, Anti-reflective and Coating equipment, Cutting equipment, Grinding equipment.
2. CAGR table in a white rounded box with a rose (#BE6E6E) border: rows Ophthalmic lenses 3％ (CAGR 24-28), High-refractive-index lenses 6％, a horizontal divider, then MCI Vision Care 6％+α (including peripheral domains). Values are printed.
3. Three country rows (China, India, Americas) each a white rounded rectangle with a rose outline and a rose tab on the left holding the label; text in each row is printed as shown.
4. Stacked column chart labelled (Sales revenue), no axis tick values printed. Categories FY08, FY20, FY22, FY24, FY26, FY28, FY30 on a horizontal baseline; FY08 is a single short light column, later columns are stacked with three series from bottom: SDC's coating materials (#F0DCDC), Functional coating materials (#DCB1B1), Processing equipment (#BE6E6E). Heights rise left to right; values are about, not printed. Columns about 3 percent wide. Grey Outlook band beneath FY28-FY30 labelled Outlook. No legend box; the three series names sit at right as text labels.
5. Logos: sdc (Surface Coating), fsi (Coating Technologies), lti (Coating Technologies), COTEC, COBURN TECHNOLOGIES, each small, above pale rose block arrows pointing down at the chart.
6. Blue (#1A76BF) thick diagonal arrow from lower-left at FY20 to upper-right beyond FY30.
7. Pink elbow connector: vertical line with dots from the bullet at the top of the right column down to M&A/tie-ups, and a second elbow from the sub-bullet down to Expansion of U.S. site with a pale rose down arrow.
8. Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right: blue swoosh with grey wordmark.
9. Icon at top left beside the title: rose square with white heart and person outline.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and headings bold black #000000 (headline text) and red #C00000 for section headers; body text black #000000 with rose #BE6E6E accent phrases and dark slate #44546A / #404040 labels; blue #1A76BF for M&A and tie-up labels; grey #595959 for footnotes and axis labels. Typefaces: Meiryo UI Bold (humanist sans-serif) for nearly all text, Meiryo UI regular for plain body lines, and Aptos Narrow for year labels. Title about 4.1 percent of slide height; section headings about 2.0 to 2.2 percent; body and bullet text about 1.7 to 1.9 percent; captions about 1.5 percent. Pills and bordered boxes use rounded corners with soft white outlines; the grey panel is flat #D9D9D9 at 70 percent opacity. No shadows except a subtle shadow on the pink banner text.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Review: Many pages are very dense with small text, as on slides 4, 5, 25 and 29, and several have rough craft: the untranslated Japanese label and the ROIC row colliding with the footer on slide 3, and low-contrast white-on-pink banner headlines on slides 3 and 9.
- What would change the score: Split the densest pages (slides 4, 5, 25, 29, 30) into a lead message with one visual each, and replace the empty divider pages 15, 16, 18 and 20 and the sparse closing page 36 with an agenda that carries a takeaway.

**Layout**

Three-zone layout on a white slide: a left vertical value-chain column (about x 0.6 to 9.5%), a dashed-outline panel holding MR and coatings text and a bar chart (about x 11 to 42.7%), and a right area with a world map and regional callouts (about x 39 to 99%). The section number 3 sits in a dark blue tab at top-left, the title beside a pink icon tile, the Mitsui Chemicals logo and date box at top-right, with a pink banner headline beneath; a copyright line sits bottom-left and the bottom-right is a pink arrow banner. No slide number is printed.

**Visuals**

Vertical chevron value chain on the left in dusty rose tones, with stacked stages: Lens manufacturers, Lens polymerization process, Optical labs, Processing, Glasses retailers, Consumers. A dashed rounded-rectangle panel (rose dashes) encloses the MR business and coatings sections. Logos: MR by Mitsui Chemicals (top of panel); sdc, fsi, lti, COTEC, COBURN TECHNOLOGIES (in a row below Coatings & equipment business); James Robinson (Specialty Ingredients) boxed under Adopting new tech; flo logo boxed under Tie-up. Bar chart: vertical bars, rose-to-pale gradient, no printed values, labelled FY16, FY20, FY24, FY28, FY30 on a horizontal axis; y-axis titled Business-wide sales revenue with no units or range printed; about heights relative to each other: FY16 about 25%, FY20 about 35%, FY24 about 55%, FY28 about 80%, FY30 about 100% of the plot height; FY28 and FY30 bars are the outlook period, marked by a translucent white Outlook label box at the bottom-right of the plot. Map: grey line-art world map on white with no fill, with rose tab callouts China, India and Americas and two rose curved arrows, one pointing down-left near Africa and one pointing down-right near South America. Mitsui Chemicals logo top-right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in black bold sans-serif (Meiryo UI Bold, rounded-gothic look) at about 4.1% of slide height. Banner headline white bold sans-serif with soft drop shadow on rose band. Body text dark grey #595959 bold sans-serif at about 2.2% of slide height; section headings MR business and Coatings & equipment business in black bold at about 3.0%. Accents in rose #BE6E6E and dusty rose #D79191; pale pink #F0DCDC bars and boxes; section number on dark blue #0B2A6F tab. Panel border dashed rose #C0787A, rounded corners, about 3 pt. Chevrons and arrows flat with no shadows or gradients except the chart bars, which fade from rose #B96A6A at the top to pale #F0DCDC at the base. Copyright line in grey at about 1.3%.

### Slide 6

**Text**

4 / Life Care: Vision Care / Mitsui Chemicals / Dec. 17, 2025 / Pursuing the development of new materials and technology to achieve continuous business growth / High strength / Photo- / chromic / Eco-friendly / New technologies / & fields / for AR/VR & / others / MR™ / by Mitsui Chemicals / SDC Technologies / Addition of functions / Expansion of realms / ◆ New high-strength brand: Expanding sales of MR-8™plus / • Impact-resistant lenses have a high market share in the U.S. / • Ensuring the brand offers adequate strength as an impact-resistant lens, while also seeking to balance this with optical properties / ◆ Photochromic materials and technologies / • Acquiring photochromic dye technology from James Robinson Speciality Ingredients, providing high-quality, high-performance photochromic systems that combine dyes with coating materials and processing equipment / ◆ Eco-friendly materials and technologies: DoGreen™ initiative / • Expanding sales of plant-derived brands (MR-160DG™, MR-174™ & others) / • Efforts to develop & commercialize technology for more efficient lens manufacturing processes / • Pursuing the chemical recycling of MR™ lens materials based on effective use of lens swarf and waste lenses / Raw materials / Disposal / Lenses / Carbon-neutral by 2050 / Support for developing sustainable eyewear products / Substantial waste reduction / Production / Product / Processing / ◆ New technologies and materials & technologies for new fields: AR/VR & others / • Tapping into digital technology to enhance development of next-gen coating materials and technology through our tie-up with flō Optics / • Developing hard coating materials offering high clarity and abrasion resistance for AR/VR applications / • Pursuing the development of high-performance materials for other fields, including automotive and electronic device applications / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

Single-slide content layout with a full-width title bar across the top: a navy chevron section tab with the number 4 at the far left, a small heart-and-person icon, the title Life Care: Vision Care in bold black sans-serif beside it, and the Mitsui Chemicals logo with a dark grey date box Dec. 17, 2025 at top right. Below a pink banner spanning the full width, the left ~40% holds a 2-axis matrix diagram with a rising vertical axis and a horizontal axis, and the right ~55% holds a stacked column of red diamond-bulleted headings with grey bullets, with a small cycle diagram sitting in the lower-right of the text column. The copyright line sits bottom-left and there is no visible slide-number text other than the 4 in the section tab.

**Visuals**

Left: a 2-axis matrix. A vertical axis arrow labelled Addition of functions (rotated, pinkish #BE6E6E) and a horizontal axis arrow labelled Expansion of realms, both drawn as thick #BE6E6E lines with solid triangular arrowheads. A soft pink-to-white gradient panel fills the matrix area, fading to white toward the lower-left and right edge. Four semi-transparent (opacity 0.70) #BE6E6E rhombus shapes: High strength (upper-left), Photo-chromic (upper-middle), Eco-friendly (centre), New technologies & fields for AR/VR & others (lower-right), each carrying white bold sans-serif labels. Thin #BE6E6E connector lines run from High strength and Photo-chromic to the right-hand headings, from Eco-friendly horizontally to its heading, and from New technologies down-right to the AR/VR heading. Logos: the MR™ mark with by Mitsui Chemicals beneath it in blue, and the SDC Technologies logo in blue with grey Technologies below. Right-hand middle: a DoGreen™ green handwritten-style logo with a green underline stroke sits in the Eco-friendly heading line. Lower-right: a flat cycle diagram of rounded navy #0B2E8A pills with white bold labels: Raw materials (top-left), Disposal (top-middle), Lenses (top-right), Production (bottom-left), Product (bottom-middle), Processing (bottom-right). Grey #A6A6A6 thick bars form a rectangular loop with a grey arrow pointing left from Disposal toward Raw materials. The inner rectangle is pale sage #CFD7A9 at about 60% opacity with a darker sage #CDD4A4 band in the middle, holding grey labels Carbon-neutral by 2050, Support for developing sustainable eyewear products and Substantial waste reduction. Top-right of the slide: Mitsui Chemicals logo with a blue swoosh and dark grey wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a pink #DCB1B1 full-width title banner. White background. Title and bold headings: title is black (#000000) bold sans-serif, about 4.1% of slide height, headings red #C00000 bold at about 2.6%. Body bullets are grey #595959 bold sans-serif at about 2.2%. Banner text is white bold sans-serif at about 3.3% on a pink #DCB1B1 band with a faint drop shadow. Accent colour is muted rose #BE6E6E for axes, diamonds and axis labels; navy #0B2E8A for section tab and cycle pills; dark grey #595959 date box. Shapes are flat with no visible gradients other than the matrix panel; diamonds have no outline; cycle pills have full rounded ends; connector lines are thin, about 0.3% of slide height, rose #BE6E6E.

### Slide 7

**Text**

5 / Wellness Solutions ：Agrochemicals / Dec. 17, 2025 / Maximizing value of active ingredients positioned / as growth drivers and accelerating their global rollouts / Doubling profit, with a / focus on growth drivers / CAGR 24–28 / Global market 2％ / MCLS 6％ / Growth drivers 7％ / (Gross profit) / Four growth / driver ingredients: / 60％ / FY19 / FY24 / FY28 / FY30 / Outlook / Dinotefuran, TENEBENAL™, / Flupyrimin, Glufosinate-P / Considering production / increases in line with demand / Accelerating rollouts to additional regional markets and applications / with a registration drive to maximize value of active ingredients / Registered countries for crop solutions / FY19 / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY24 / Dinotefuran / Brazil / TENEBENAL™ / Japan, South Korea / China, Philippines, Indonesia / India / Vietnam, Thailand / Flupyrimin / Japan / India / Glufosinate-P / Grew domestic sales & bolstered overseas rollout / Registrations for TENEBENAL™ / vs. December 2024 / Crop solutions / Life solutions / Registered / 6➡8 / 22➡23 / Applied / 7➡5 / 1➡1 / Bolstering development of formulations and mixtures for each active ingredient / in line with the needs of various countries, regions and target markets / Breaking down markets by region, crop and pest to better analyze needs / Developing formulations and compounds able to differentiate themselves in target markets / Developing future growth drivers for beyond 2030 / Fleshing out our pipeline with both chemical and biological crop protection products / Actively conducting M&A both in Japan and abroad in pursuit of further business / expansion / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

Three-part slide: a 5-column header strip with section number, icon, title and logo; a pink tagline band across the full width; a left rounded panel (about 4% to 29% of width) holding a bar chart with CAGR box, and a right area holding a registration table at upper right with a small registrations table beside it and three text blocks stacked below. Title is top-left at about 11% x and 2.4% y; the copyright footer sits bottom-left at 0.2% x and 98.8% y. There is no visible slide number beyond the section badge '5'.

**Visuals**

1) Column chart inside left rounded panel: stacked bars, two-tone (dark #BE6E6E bottom segment, light #DCB1B1 top segment) for FY19, FY24, FY28, FY30 on x-axis labelled Outlook (grey bar beneath FY28-FY30); no numeric axis labels; y-axis label '(Gross profit)'; bars increase left to right, FY30 bar has a light-pink to dark-pink gradient fill; values not printed (heights about: FY19 small, FY24 about 2x FY19, FY28 about 3x, FY30 tallest). Blue arrow (#1A76BF) rises diagonally from lower-left at FY19 to upper-right above FY30. 2) CAGR box: outlined rounded rectangle with title CAGR 24–28 and rows Global market 2%, MCLS 6%, Growth drivers 7% (7 in red). 3) Table 'Registered countries for crop solutions' with column headers FY19, FY20, FY21, FY22, (unlabelled blank column), FY24 and rows: Dinotefuran (Brazil in FY19), TENEBENAL™ (Japan, South Korea in FY20; China, Philippines, Indonesia in FY21; India in FY22; Vietnam, Thailand in FY24), Flupyrimin (Japan in FY19; India in FY22), Glufosinate-P (merged cell across FY20-FY24: Grew domestic sales & bolstered overseas rollout). 4) Small table 'Registrations for TENEBENAL™' with columns vs. December 2024, Crop solutions, Life solutions; rows Registered (6➡8 crop, 22➡23 life) and Applied (7➡5 crop, 1➡1 life); the 8 and 23 are red bold; a red dashed rectangle outlines the 6➡8 cell; a red dashed arrow with a left-pointing red arrowhead runs from the Registered row toward the Vietnam, Thailand cell. Grey arrow glyphs (➡) sit between values. 5) Logo: Mitsui Chemicals swoosh mark in blue with dark grey wordmark, upper right. 6) Section icon: white heart-and-person outline icon on pink tile at upper left. 7) Several small unlabelled images (64 and 70 pixel squares) with no legible content.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a pink tagline band; left panel light pink with rounded corners; navy section badge and grey chevron at top left; date tag dark grey. Title text black MeiryoUI-Bold sans-serif, about 4.1% of slide height (size of capitals about 2.9%). Tagline white bold about 3.3%. Section headings black bold about 2.6%; bullet/sub-lines in mauve #BE6E6E bold about 2.2%; body text regular about 2.0%. Red #C00000 for left panel headline, 60%, 8, 23, 7 and considering-production text. Table headers white bold on #DCB1B1 cells; body cells #F0DCDC at 60% opacity with white gutters about 0.5%. Left panel border pinkish #DCB1B1 rounded. Shapes flat, no shadows except a faint shadow under the header rule. Copyright in grey #595959 regular about 1.3%.

### Slide 8

**Text**

6 / Wellness Solutions ：Agrochemicals / Mitsui Chemicals / Dec. 17, 2025 / Speeding up business growth through early achievement of positive effects from M&A / Agrochemical business acquired from Meiji Seika Pharma seeing steady growth above the initial plan / • Following integration of the sites of both companies when the acquisition was completed in January 2022, integrated the / companies themselves and carried out a reorganization a year later / • Established the BSRC in April 2023 with the objective of strengthening biosolutions research based on infrastructure for / naturally derived drug discovery, to further develop Meiji Seika Pharma’s strengths. Also expanded open innovation / • Measures to achieve synergies are progressing according to plan. Sales synergies in particular have been achieved earlier than / planned, worth in excess of 1 billion yen of costs per year / *1 Biological Solutions Research Center / BSRC Shinkiba Satellite Lab / Growth in ZAXA Liquid*2 / Initial plan revised upward due to effective use of / MCI’s domestic sales resources / ⇒ Planning to increase the Group’s domestic production / capacity for preparations / ZAXA Liquid / (Volume: kL) / FY23 / FY24 / FY25 / FY28 / FY30 / Plan at acquisition / Results + Outlook / Leveraging infrastructure at the Kitakami Factory / (factory acquired from Meiji Seika Pharma) / Development of ability to serve as our second major active / ingredient plant after the Omuta Works / Launch of probenazole and flupyrimin production; / achievement of business continuity planning in procurement / ⇒ Investment in equipment to reduce production costs, and in / turn further boost competitiveness, is proceeding smoothly / Rolling out a wide range of products using probenazole / and flupyrimin active ingredients produced at the Kitakami Factory / *2 Glufosinate-p (preparation containing an active ingredient originally from Meiji Seika Pharma) / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a thin header band at the top holding a blue-and-grey chevron with the section number 6 at the left, a pink icon tile, the title at upper left, and the Mitsui Chemicals logo and a dark grey date box at upper right. A full-width pink banner carries the centred headline below the header; below it, a left-aligned bold red question-style heading and three bullets run to the left, with the BSRC photo and caption at upper right. The lower half is two columns: the left has a ZAXA Liquid bar chart panel in a rounded pink box, the right has a Kitakami Factory text block above a rounded pink box of five product photos with a caption; a footnote and copyright line sit at the bottom left.

**Visuals**

Bar chart (ZAXA Liquid, Volume in kL, no axis numbers printed): categories FY23, FY24, FY25, FY28, FY30 on the horizontal axis; no vertical axis scale or values are printed, so bar heights are approximate. Series 1 Plan at acquisition in light pink #F1B9B9 (about FY23 low, FY24 slightly higher, FY25 mid, FY28 mid-high, FY30 about 60 percent of results). Series 2 Results + Outlook in dark rose #BE6E6E (FY23 about 45 percent of max, FY24 about 55 percent, FY25 about 65 percent, FY28 about 85 percent, FY30 tallest, fading to a lighter top). A solid blue arrow #1A76BF rises from left of FY23 to above FY30 across the bars. Legend below the category labels: light pink square Plan at acquisition, dark rose square Results + Outlook. Photo of ZAXA Liquid herbicide bottles at right of the chart: a small bottle with red cap and a large two-litre jug with red cap and red and white label reading サンサ, label text 2.0 L, company name Mitsui Chemicals Crop & Life Solutions, on a white background with soft shadow. Photo of BSRC Shinkiba Satellite Lab: a rendered architectural perspective of a seven-storey building with a grey-brown and white facade of horizontal and vertical stripes, a rooftop sign, a leafy tree and a street at the base, shown in daylight, full view, boxed without border. Five product photos in a row inside a pink rounded box: pink-and-white bag reading オリゼメート リディア 箱粒剤; teal bag with blue label reading リディア 箱粒剤; orange bottle with label エミリ フロアブル; light blue bag reading リディア 箱粒剤 with a blue band; green-and-white bag with a rice plant illustration reading オリゼメート 粒剤. All product photos are on white backgrounds, unboxed, same height. Mitsui Chemicals logo: blue swoosh mark and dark grey wordmark at upper right. Header icon: a pink rounded tile with a white outline icon of a person inside a heart with an arrow. Small section chevron: blue #0A3A8C square with white 6 and grey chevron.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a bubble chart with bubble size for operating income to show how structural reform moves each region's invested capital and ROIC toward the target.
- Review: Many pages are very dense with small text, as on slides 4, 5, 25 and 29, and several have rough craft: the untranslated Japanese label and the ROIC row colliding with the footer on slide 3, and low-contrast white-on-pink banner headlines on slides 3 and 9.
- Review: The density suits a circulated investor document better than a spoken presentation, and the consistent frame and the strongest pages (slides 9 and 24) keep it competent and usable, with consequential weaknesses.
- Worth keeping: Keep the repeated page frame with its numbered badge, banded takeaway headline and sector color, and the clear single-idea layouts of slide 9 (reform table beside the ROIC bubble path) and slide 24 (before-and-after battery pack with photos).
- Suggested repair: Fix the legibility and fit defects: raise the contrast of the pale-pink banner headlines on slides 3 and 9, translate the Japanese label on slide 3, and move the ROIC row on slide 3 off the footer.

**Layout**

Three-part slide: a left column (about 0 to 30% of width) holds a bubble chart headline, chart and red conclusion text; a right column (about 33 to 98% of width) holds three numbered sections, each with a heading line and a grey table. Title sits top-right of the blue section tab in a header bar at top (about 0 to 8% height), with a section chevron '7' at far left, a heart icon, and the Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right; a pink full-width banner (about 9.5 to 17.4% height) carries a two-line white subtitle centred. Left-aligned table text, margins about 3 to 4% of width, copyright footer bottom left, no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Bubble chart (left) with title 'Becoming a global leader with a strong presence' in red above it. Axes: vertical axis labelled ROIC (grey), horizontal axis labelled Invested capital (grey); no numeric scale or units printed. Five pink bubbles in a descending diagonal from upper left to lower right, labelled FY28 (largest, about 6 percent by 11 percent of slide), FY27, FY26, FY25 and FY24 (smallest), with small grey arrows between consecutive bubbles pointing down-right. Bubble size legend text: 'Bubble size = operating income before special items'. Two dotted dark-pink horizontal lines cross the chart: the upper one at about 46 percent of slide height labelled 'Achieve >9%' in a red-outlined box, the lower one at about 54 percent labelled 'Achieve ROIC = WACC' in a red-outlined box. A dotted-outline empty circle at upper right of the chart with a thick dusky-rose arrow pointing up-right toward it. Below the chart, a pink downward triangle pointing to the red conclusion text. Logo row in table: Mitsui Chemicals logo (blue swoosh with dark text), a multiplication sign, SUN MEDICAL logo (blue diamond mark with blue text), a multiplication sign, Kulzer logo (green circle icon with black KULZER wordmark and small text MITSUI CHEMICALS GROUP) on a white strip, with captions beneath: 'Monomers utilizing tech from the vision care sector' under the Mitsui logo, 'Development & production' under SUN MEDICAL, 'Sales' under Kulzer. Product photograph at right of the Sales row: a black and white product box labelled ZEN CAD-4 with four pale beige CAD/CAM crown blocks in front, shown at a three-quarter angle on a light grey background, full colour pale tones with dark box, no crop. Heart-in-hands icon in pink square at top left of title and a blue chevron with white '7'.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white slide with a pale rose banner (#DCB1B1) under the header and grey table cells (#D9D9D9 at about 70 percent opacity for right-hand columns). Titles and headings black #000000, bold Meiryo UI style sans-serif, title about 4.1 percent of slide height; section headings about 2.2 percent with black labels and rose #BE6E6E accent text; body table text black about 1.8 percent. Red #C00000 for chart headline and conclusion text; blue #223F99 for Achieve labels; grey #7F7F7F for ROIC label; dark grey #404040 for year labels. Blue #0B2F7A chevron tab with white numeral 7. Grey table cells with white gutter lines, no rounded corners, no shadows, thin white borders between cells. Bubbles solid pink #DCB1B1 with no outline; dotted lines dusky rose #BE6E6E. Header has a faint grey shadow beneath the white header bar.

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a thin header band across the top containing a navy numbered tab (8) at the left, a rose icon square, the title Medical Solutions: Oral Care directly right of the icon, and the Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right above a dark grey date box. A rose-tinted full-width banner sits below the header with a two-line centred subtitle. Below it the world map fills the slide; the left-aligned bullet block sits in the lower-left and a boxed bar chart with a rounded panel sits at right. Callout boxes are placed over the map with blue arrows linking them. The copyright line is at bottom left; there is no slide-number field beyond the navy tab.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed pale rose-red world map (illustration) covering the slide, with a soft blue radial glow ellipse centred over Africa and the Atlantic. Blue semi-transparent curved arrows: one from the Sale of SUN MEDICAL box down to the U.S. flag; one from the Asia area curving left to the Europe callout; one from Asia to the Europe/Middle East area; one from the Middle East box up to the Europe area. A US flag (about 5.5% wide) sits below the first callout. An EU flag and a German flag (black, red, gold horizontal tricolour) sit side by side under the Europe callout. A bar chart titled Reducing S&GA ratio to a level below global competitors: five rose bars (#BE6E6E) labelled FY24, FY25, FY26, FY27, FY28 on the x-axis, bars descending left to right; values are not printed, so heights are about: FY24 about 85% of the chart height, FY25 about 75%, FY26 about 65%, FY27 about 55%, FY28 about 40%. A horizontal dashed dark grey line (#595959) marks Global competitors’ S&GA ratio across the chart at about 67% height. The chart has a black baseline. A rose-outlined boxed label Scope for a 15% reduction in S&GA personnel overlies the lower bars. A dashed dark navy ellipse encloses the Southeast Asia area with the label Withdrawing from inefficient areas. Four white checkmarks in the bullet list (Wingdings ✓). Logo: Mitsui Chemicals wave mark in blue and grey wordmark at top right.

**Design**

Background: White (#FFFFFF) with a pale rose-red (#F0AAAA) world map image covering the slide and a blue glow. White header and white slide ground over the pink-red map. Header title is black (#000000) MeiryoUI-Bold sans-serif, about 4.1% of slide height. Subtitle banner text white (#FFFFFF) MeiryoUI-Bold with a soft drop shadow, about 3.3% of slide height. Bullet and heading text white bold sans-serif about 3.0%. Callout and chart labels black bold sans-serif about 2.2%; chart heading red (#C00000) bold about 2.6%. Copyright line grey (#595959) regular about 1.3%. Accent colours: navy #0B3A8C tab, rose #BE6E6E panel and bars, rose banner #DCB1B1, blue #0000FF borders and arrows at 40% opacity. Callout boxes have square corners with 2 px blue (#0000FF) borders. Chart panel has rounded corners of about 3% of slide height with a #BE6E6E border. No gradients except the blue glow; no shadows except subtle text shadow in subtitle.

**Layout**

Full-width header bar across the top with a navy numbered tile '9' at left, a section icon beside the title 'Medical Solutions: Oral Care' (left-aligned, starting at about x 11%), and the Mitsui Chemicals logo with a grey date box 'Dec. 17, 2025' at top right. Below is a full-width pink banner with centred headline. The main area is a diagonal timeline of years 2017 to 2025 rising from bottom left to top right, with product images placed at left and centre; a bordered call-out box sits at right with a footnote beneath it. A red-bordered summary banner spans the bottom, and the copyright line sits at bottom left.

**Visuals**

A rising pale-pink translucent diagonal arrow (#EFADAD at about 40% opacity) runs from bottom left (near 2017) to upper right (near the 2025 entry), with its head at about x 62%, y 18%. Photographs: (1) a complete denture photograph (pink gums, yellow-white teeth) boxed, top centre, at about x 46.5% y 17.2% w 11.2% h 15.5%; (2) a grey 3D-printed artificial teeth model with yellow front teeth and small holes, cut out, labelled 'Artificial teeth', at x 22.6% y 23% w 11.6% h 11.7%; (3) a pink denture base cutout labelled 'Denture bases', at x 11.8% y 31.6% w 10.6% h 11.1%; (4) mouthguards (blue upper, white lower) labelled 'Mouthguards', at x 0% y 37.9% w 13.3% h 23.8%; (5) red 3D-printed metal casting molds labelled 'Metal casting molds', at x 50.4% y 67.8% w 13.1% h 16.4%; (6) a white and orange 3D printer machine with a small blue screen, cut out inside the call-out box, at x 67.2% y 48% w 8.3% h 21%. Dotted red leader lines connect labels to products and the 2020 entry. Logos: Mitsui Chemicals logo (blue swoosh with grey wordmark) at top right, and a pink square icon with a person-in-heart symbol at top left. Shape details: two pale-pink rounded rectangles (#EFADAD at 20% opacity) behind the call-out text lines at x 75.1% y 52.1% and x 75.1% y 60.5%, each 23.1% by 7%.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF). Header tile navy #0B2F8A with white bold numeral '9'; chevron tab in light grey beside it. Section icon tile pink #DCB1B1. Title black (#000000) bold sans-serif (Meiryo UI Bold), about 4.1% of slide height. Pink banner #DCB1B1 with white bold headline (Meiryo UI Bold, 3.3%) and soft shadow. Year numbers black bold sans-serif (Meiryo UI Bold, about 3% of height). Descriptions black bold Meiryo UI, about 2.2%. Red accents #C00000 for 2025 entry, 'Japan’s first' and 'world’s first' (underlined), and the bottom summary text (Meiryo UI Bold, 3%). Date box background dark grey #595959 with white bold text. Call-out box outline #C00000 about 0.3% width, rounded corners with small radius, fill white. Footnote and body text Meiryo UI regular. Bottom summary box has a red #C00000 rounded border about 0.6% thick, pale pink fill (#DCB1B1 at 28% opacity). Copyright text grey #595959, small Meiryo UI regular.

**Layout**

Single slide with a full-width pink banner headline under the header rule, a row of two white statistic boxes, a three-step grey band with pink step boxes flowing left to right, a red caption under the band, and a large dotted rounded frame below holding a left column (DNA Chip Research and Mitsui Chemicals logos with capability text), a centre column of outcome text, and a right speech-bubble callout with a small bar chart. Title is top-left beside a section number chevron; the logo is top-right and the date sits on a grey tab beneath it. The copyright line is bottom-left with no slide number.

**Visuals**

One chart: a pale pink upward trend arrow over three vertical bars labelled on the x-axis FY23, FY24 and FY30, titled 'Testing & diagnosis business sales revenue' in grey, with no printed axis units, ranges or values. Bars are about 15% (FY23), about 55% (FY24) and about 100% (FY30) of the plot height, filled dusky rose #BE6E6E with the FY30 bar shaded with a white-to-pink gradient at its top; no legend; no highlighted points. Logos: DNA Chip Research Inc. (cyan and black stylised DNA symbol with 'DNA Chip Research Inc.' wordmark in dark grey) appears twice, at the left column and at the top of the callout; Mitsui Chemicals (blue swoosh with grey wordmark) appears at top-right and bottom-left. A red-rose multiplication sign × sits between the two company blocks. A pale pink block arrow points right from the left column to the centre column. Three white rounded step boxes contain text; a thick rose outline surrounds Step 2. Two thin light-grey outlined hollow arrows connect the step boxes. A pale pink downward triangular arrow points from the band to the red caption. Small unlabelled raster images sit near the callout corner and near the bottom-right footnote.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Title black #000000, bold Meiryo UI-style Japanese gothic sans-serif, about 4.1% of slide height for the main title and 3.7% for the subtitle. Top banner in dusky pink #DCB1B1 with white bold centred text (about 3.3% of height) carrying a soft dark drop shadow. Section number '10' white on a navy #0B2E8A chevron. Date 'Dec. 17, 2025' white on dark grey #595959 tab. Statistic and step text black #000000 at about 2.2% of height; accents dusky rose #BE6E6E for 'a rise in infectious disease', 'personalized cancer treatments', 'High hopes for new solutions', FY labels and pink callout heading; the red accent #C00000 is used for the statistic '8', the caption and the synergy heading. Step tags pale rose #DCB1B1 with white bold labels about 2.6% high. Step boxes pale pink #F0DCDC with about 1.3% corner radius; Step 2 box has a thick #BE6E6E outline. Grey band #F2F2F2 behind steps. Statistic boxes white with light grey #D9D9D9 border about 0.4% thick and rounded corners. Bottom frame and callout use dotted rose outlines (#BE6E6E and #C9A0A0). Pill 'Jointly developed items' filled #DCB1B1 with white text. Copyright line grey #595959 about 1.3% of height. No gradients except the chart bar and the banner shadow.

### Slide 13

**Text**

Medical Solutions： DNA Chip Research
11
Dec. 17, 2025
• Acquired DNA Chip Research as a wholly owned subsidiary in June 2025. Invested human resources, including as / president, in pursuit of post-merger integration
• Mainstay business the Lung Cancer Compact Panel™* offers excellent detection sensitivity and can also be used for / cytological specimens
➡ Helps to ensure prompt administration of appropriate drugs by enabling testing of patients who had been unable to undergo / multiplex companion diagnostics
• Leveraging our business foundations to expand the business by rolling it out overseas. Currently also devoting efforts to / developing new diagnostic content for diseases other than lung cancer

⚫ Considering the inclusion of / additional genes and drugs / ⚫ Bolstering sales and marketing / activities / ⚫ Overseas rollout leveraging / our business foundations

FY2028

*Lung Cancer Compact Panel™ Dx Multiplex Companion Diagnostic System

**Layout**

Single full-width slide with a thin white header band (top 8%) holding the navy section badge with page number 11, a pink icon, the title at left and the Mitsui Chemicals logo and grey date box at right. Below, a rose-outlined box spans the full width (about 2% margins) holding four bullets; beneath it the slide is split into two columns: left column (Lung Cancer Compact Panel pictogram, description, and five Features boxes) and right column (growth bar chart, bullets and a Further increases statement), separated by a dashed vertical divider at about 50% width. Titles sit above each column in rose header bars; text is left aligned. Copyright line at bottom left and footnote at bottom right; no slide number other than the badge.

**Visuals**

1) Pictogram diagram at upper left: about 30 small multicoloured standing person figures in a row (blue, green, yellow, purple, orange, red, cyan repeated) above a light blue box reading Multiplex companion diagnostics and a pink box reading Lung Cancer Compact Panel™, etc. with a pink arrow pointing from the pink box to the blue box. Below, black connector lines drop from the blue box to seven columns labelled EGFR (green), ALK (yellow), KRAS (cyan), BRAF (purple), ROS1 (blue), RET (orange), MET (red). Each column shows coloured person figures (EGFR about 4 green, ALK 2 yellow, KRAS 2 cyan, BRAF 1 purple, ROS1 1 blue, RET 1 orange, MET 1 red), a down arrow in the same colour, and an outlined pill bottle in the same colour. 2) Bar chart titled with the header Growth of the Lung Cancer Compact Panel business (no chart title in plot). Vertical bar chart, category axis FY2023, FY2024, FY2025, FY2028 with an axis break marks between FY2025 and FY2028, so FY2028 is not to scale. Value axis has no visible numbers or units (value labels are hidden under a white box). Estimated bar heights (about, no values printed): FY2023 about 1,900; FY2024 about 5,600; FY2025 about 8,400; FY2028 about 13,000. No legend; no highlighted points. A thick rose trend arrow (#BE6E6E) rises from the top of the FY2023 bar to an arrowhead above FY2028 and is labelled Number of tests on the rise in bold red. 3) Five rounded feature boxes (light pink fill, navy outline) stacked at left. 4) Rose chevron arrows: one pointing right between Features list and the description column, one pointing right between the bullets and the Further statement. 5) Mitsui Chemicals logo (teal-blue swoosh and grey wordmark) top right. 6) Icon at top left: white heart-and-person outline with arrow on dusty pink square. 7) Navy badge with white page number 11 and grey chevron.

**Design**

Background: Plain solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with a thin light grey shadow under the header band. Title and page number in black #000000 (title) and white #FFFFFF (number on navy). Body text black #000000 with emphasis in red #C00000 bold. Section headers white bold on rose #BE6E6E. Accent colours: rose #BE6E6E for headers, arrows and boxed frame; navy #0B3A8A for page badge; bar blues #5B9BD5 and #4889C9; feature boxes fill #F0DCDC with navy #1F3A5F outline. Typeface Meiryo UI (humanist sans-serif, Japanese gothic); title bold about 4.1% of slide height, body about 2.6% of slide height, small labels about 1.5% to 2.2%. Frame border 2 px rose; feature boxes have small corner radius (about 0.5% of width) and no shadow; dashed grey divider; trend arrow and chevrons flat with no gradients or shadows.

Mobility Solutions Business Strategy / for VISION 2030 / Dec. 17, 2025 / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

A single title block sits left of centre, with the title starting at about 16% from the left edge and vertically centred near 45% to 58% of the height. A pair of chevron marks sits just left of the title. The Mitsui Chemicals logo is in the top-right corner, with the date directly beneath it. The copyright line sits in the bottom-left corner. There is no slide number and no footer beyond the copyright line.

**Visuals**

Two solid dark-blue chevron marks (double right-pointing chevrons, like a fast-forward symbol) sit to the left of the title. The Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right is a blue and grey swoosh mark beside the grey wordmark 'Mitsui Chemicals'.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a soft grey blurred chevron shading on the right side. Plain white slide. Chevron marks are solid #072884 with no outline. The right-side chevron shading is a soft grey gradient with a blurred edge. No corner radii, no rules, no borders.

**Text**

Mobility Solutions Business Strategy for VISION 2030

Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

A white header strip across the top holds a navy numbered tab '1' with a chevron edge at far left, a presenter icon, the bold black title at left, and the Mitsui Chemicals logo with a grey date box at top right. Below a blue-grey band with a centred two-line heading, the body is a three-column arrangement: a left column of grey heading boxes with coloured tiles, a middle column of grey text boxes, and a right column bar chart, with a large blue arrow running across the middle from left to right. A dark slate footer bar with two centred white lines spans the bottom 10 percent, with the copyright line at its bottom left; margins are about 2 percent on the left and right, and there is no slide number beyond the '1' tab.

**Visuals**

Bar chart, untitled, with three vertical bars rising from a shared baseline at the bottom of the bars, drawn to scale (heights proportional to 53, 72 and 93). Bar 1 is labelled FY25 Outlook, value 53B yen, ROIC 10%. Bar 2 is labelled FY28 Target, value 72B yen, ROIC 12%. Bar 3 is labelled FY30 Target, value 93B yen, ROIC 15%. No y-axis, gridlines or legend are printed; the values are the printed labels. Bars carry a vertical gradient from pale blue at the top to dark teal at the bottom. A semi-transparent yellow (#FFD966 at 70 percent opacity) upward-pointing arrow overlays the bars, starting near the top of FY25 bar's lower left area and ending with its head above FY30 bar, highlighting the growth trend. Also a large right-pointing block arrow in blue-grey #689BB0 behind the middle column, its head at the right tip pointing toward the chart, with a soft horizontal gradient from transparent on the left fading into the arrow body. A small presenter icon in a teal square at top left (a person at a desk with speech-wave lines). A Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right: a blue swoosh mark with the wordmark. The 'Ideal vision' label is a grey-blue wordmark inside a white box at left of the band.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background; navy tab #072884 at top left; header bottom edge has a soft grey shadow. Title in bold black #000000 Meiryo UI-style humanist sans-serif, about 4.1 percent of slide height. Body text in bold dark grey #404040 Meiryo UI-style sans-serif, about 3.0 percent of slide height. Band fill #689BB0 with white bold heading text about 4.1 percent. Accent colours: teal #3A7A96, blue #2F5597, green #548235, rust #C55A11, orange #EE8640, grey #D9D9D9 boxes, slate footer #44546A. Shapes are flat with square corners except the Elastomers, Composite, Solutions and Solutions & new business tiles which have rounded corners (about 1 percent radius) and a faint drop shadow. Text in tiles and the band has a soft drop shadow. Arrow and bars are flat or gradient-filled with no outlines.

**Layout**

Section divider with a plain white background. The title block sits left of centre, vertically centred at about 45% to 58% of the slide height, starting at x 16.4%. Two blue double-chevron marks sit to its left. The date is at top right under the logo, and the copyright line is at bottom left. No slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Two solid dark blue double-chevron (chevron-arrow) shapes, filled #072884, placed side by side to the left of the title, each about 3.8% wide and 11.1% tall, with no outline. The Mitsui Chemicals logo sits at top right: a blue and light-blue swoosh emblem next to dark grey 'Mitsui Chemicals' wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a soft grey chevron-shaped shadow band on the right side. Plain white background. Date is bold black #000000 MeiryoUI-Bold about 2.0% of slide height. Copyright line is regular grey #595959 MeiryoUI about 1.3% of slide height. Accent shapes are solid #072884 with sharp corners and no shadows. The right-hand chevron band has a soft grey shadow edge with a gradient fade into white.

### Slide 19

**Text**

2 / Mobility Solutions Business Earnings and Targets / Mitsui Chemicals / Dec. 17, 2025 / Driving further growth with efforts focused on the shift to growth markets and differentiation / (単位：億円) / ■ Operating income before special items / EBITDA / 93 / 72 / 53 / 58 / 55 / 49 / 33 / Volume / +6.0 / Elastomers(+) / Terms / of trade / (5.5) / TAFMER™/PP compounds(-) / Foreign exchange impact(-) / Costs, etc. / (2.6) / New plant(-) / Business / restructuring(+) / Return from / previous / investments / & Organic / growth / Increase in / fixed costs / (New / investments) / FY21 / Results / FY22 / Results / FY23 / Results / FY24 / Results / FY25 / Outlook / FY28 / Target / FY30 / Target / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

Header band across the top with a navy numbered tab at left, a blue icon tile, the title at upper left and the Mitsui Chemicals logo with date box at upper right. A full-width teal banner sits below the header carrying the message line. Below it a single waterfall-style bar chart fills the content area on a shared baseline near 88% of slide height, with a unit label and legend at upper left, chart labels beneath the baseline, and a copyright line at the bottom left. No slide number other than the navy '2' tab.

**Visuals**

Combined waterfall and bar chart with EBITDA line, titled by the slide title, unit (単位：億円, hundreds of millions of yen; no axis is drawn and no axis range is printed). Bars with printed values: FY21 Results 33, FY22 Results 49, FY23 Results 58, FY24 Results 55, FY25 Outlook 53, FY28 Target 72, FY30 Target 93. Waterfall steps between FY24 and FY25: Volume +6.0 (orange bar, callout 'Elastomers(+)'), Terms of trade (5.5) (pink bar, callout 'TAFMER™/PP compounds(-)' and 'Foreign exchange impact(-)'), Costs, etc. (2.6) (pink bar, callout 'New plant(-)' and 'Business restructuring(+)'). Between FY25 and FY28: Return from previous investments and Organic growth (blue bar, about +19 units tall relative to the scale, no printed value) and Increase in fixed costs (New investments) (pink bar, no printed value). Series 1 is the light blue-grey and mid teal bars as listed. Series 2 is an EBITDA line in #689BB0 with round markers, drawn as a smooth curve through the FY21 to FY24 markers and marker dots at FY25 Outlook, FY28 Target area and FY30 region, with no printed EBITDA values. Legend at upper left: a teal square labelled Operating income before special items and a teal line with two circle ends labelled EBITDA. Highlighted points: FY25 Outlook value 53 printed in red #C00000, FY28 and FY30 values printed in navy #003399. No axis lines except a black horizontal baseline. Logo: Mitsui Chemicals wordmark with blue swoosh at upper right. Icon: a white presentation-and-signal pictogram on a teal tile at left of the title.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a light grey shadow under the header. Navy #072884 numbered tab at far left of header. Titles in black #000000 MeiryoUI-Bold sans-serif, about 4.1% of slide height. Banner text white #FFFFFF MeiryoUI-Bold with a soft drop shadow, about 3.3% of slide height. Body and chart labels in grey #595959 and #3B3838, MeiryoUI sans-serif, about 2.2 to 3.0% of slide height. Chart numbers in Calibri-Bold, #404040 for historical values, #C00000 for FY25, #003399 for FY28 and FY30, sizes about 3.7 to 5.2% of height. Accent colours: teal #689BB0, light blue-grey #ABC9D5, blue #5B9BD5, orange, and pink #FF7C80 style red for negative steps. Shapes are flat with square corners and no gradients. Callouts are thin grey #7F7F7F rectangles with straight pointer lines, about 1 pt stroke. Date box is a dark grey #595959 rectangle with white bold text.

### Slide 21

**Text**

3 / Mobility Solutions Business Strategy / Dec. 17, 2025 / Combining seeds-driven market development / with the provision of solutions and needs-driven materials / Provision of materials / Elastomers / Composite materials / Solutions & new business / Social challenge / Identify social challenge; explore / themes for commercialization / Select theme; set and / verify hypothesis / Build business / model / Commer- / cialize / Future mobility / New / business / Ethylene / Propylene / Other / monomers / MCI / products / Procured / raw / materials / Develop- / ment / needs / Polymer design and / catalyst technologies / Polymerization / process technologies / New polymerization processes / Technologies / (formulation, mixing, compounds) / Local production for local / consumption setups in each region / Offering concepts / for modular components / Develop- / ment / needs / Functional / support / for / concept / develop- / ment / Collaboration / Utilization of other materials / Business partners / Collaboration / Seeking to bolster business by ensuring the three pillars leverage / the strengths linked by the value chain and collaborate organically / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

Full-width slide with a white header band holding a navy section chevron with the number 3 at left, a small icon tile, and the bold black title at top left; the Mitsui Chemicals logo sits top right above a dark grey date box. Below a steel-blue banner, a three-column diagram fills the middle: Elastomers (left), Composite materials (centre) and Solutions & new business (right), each a coloured framed panel with a header bar and white inner panel, joined by arrows. Two blue-bordered bottom boxes sit under the outer columns with collaboration arrows, and a dark slate footer bar carries the two-line conclusion, with a small copyright line at bottom left.

**Visuals**

Diagram. Three large panels. (1) Elastomers panel, blue #2F5597: inside, two grey rounded boxes 'Ethylene Propylene' and 'Other monomers' joined by a navy plus sign, then a navy multiplication cross, then three stacked light-blue rounded bars 'Polymer design and catalyst technologies', 'Polymerization process technologies', and a dotted-outline pale-blue bar 'New polymerization processes'. (2) Composite materials panel, green #548235: grey boxes 'MCI products' and 'Procured raw materials' joined by a navy plus sign, a navy multiplication cross, then three stacked light-green bars 'Technologies (formulation, mixing, compounds)', 'Local production for local consumption setups in each region', 'Offering concepts for modular components'. (3) Solutions & new business panel, orange #C55A11: grey box 'Social challenge' above a navy down arrow feeding an inverted funnel of four tiers, shaded from pale peach to deep orange: 'Identify social challenge; explore themes for commercialization', 'Select theme; set and verify hypothesis', 'Build business model', 'Commer-cialize'. Two navy circular-arrow loop icons sit between funnel tiers. Below the funnel, a grey band holds an orange filled box 'Future mobility' and a dashed-orange outlined box 'New business'. Logos: ARRK in blue wordmark and KYOWA in red outlined wordmark, set over a pale blue-grey curved arc. Arrows: thick blue elbow connector from Elastomers top to the right panel top, green elbow connector from Composite top to the right panel top; three green left-pointing arrows between Elastomers and Composite labelled 'Development needs'; three orange left-pointing arrows between Composite and Solutions labelled 'Development needs'; one orange left-pointing arrow labelled 'Functional support for concept development'. Blue double-headed arrows (four each) with 'Collaboration' labels link the bottom boxes to the Composite and Solutions panels. Logo: Mitsui Chemicals swoosh mark in blue with dark grey wordmark, top right. Icon: white presentation/person icon on a steel-blue tile, top left, next to the navy chevron with number 3.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 22

**Text**

4 / Mobility Solutions Business Strategy / (1) Elastomers / Dec. 17, 2025 / Cultivating markets and pursuing differentiation by leveraging polymers with unique strengths / via the combination of raw materials, catalysts and production technologies / TAFMER™ / Alpha-olefin copolymer / MITSUI EPT™ / Ethylene-Propylene Terpolymer / LUCANT™ / Ethylene-alpha-olefin co- / oligomer / Business strategy / Continually launch / differentiated products / Supply raw materials for / composite materials / Develop new polymers / and establish / polymerization / technologies / China / Local / production / for local / consumption / Sharing / differentiation / technologies / Japan / - Mother plant / (R&D site and production of / differentiated products) / - Considering a next-generation / elastomer plant / Singapore / ‒ Global supply using our large-scale plant / ‒ Operation of new plant / TAFMER™ / MITSUI EPT™ / LUCANT™ / Achieving further growth and major advances as a front-runner / by continually bringing new products to market / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

Full-width white header band at top (about 8% of height) with a blue section tab reading 4 at far left, the slide title Mobility Solutions Business Strategy above the subtitle (1) Elastomers beside a light-blue icon, the Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right and a grey date box beneath it. Below the header, a full-width rounded blue tagline banner holds three white product cards centred in a row; underneath, a narrow left column (about 2.5% to 25.7% of width) holds the Business strategy panel, and the world map fills the right side with China, Japan and Singapore callouts. A dark slate footer band runs across the bottom 10.5% with a centred two-line message and a small copyright line at bottom left. No slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Grey world map (raster, 1984 by 939 px) across the right two-thirds of the slide with curved light-blue arrows and translucent overlays. Left panel contains a 2x2 photo collage (about 537 by 338 px): top-left a car on a road with an electric-vehicle charging station and a digital overlay, top-right the rear seats and trunk of a white concept car with tan seats, bottom-left a battery-electric vehicle chassis with a battery pack, bottom-right white pouch packages with a recycling symbol. Five light-blue curved arrows: one from Japan arcing over Asia to Europe with its head pointing left, one from Japan arcing to North America with its head pointing right, one from Singapore to Africa with head pointing up-left, one from Singapore up to India with head pointing up, one from Singapore to South America with head pointing right. Pink translucent ellipse over China, yellow translucent elongated ellipse over Japan and Southeast Asia. Coloured dots: red (TAFMER), green (MITSUI EPT), yellow (LUCANT) — one green dot over China; one red, one green and one yellow dot at Japan; one red dot near Singapore. Legend at bottom right: red circle TAFMER™, green circle MITSUI EPT™, yellow circle LUCANT™.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with light-blue tagline banner #689BB0, pale blue left panel #ABC9D5, dark slate-teal boxes #3C6474, footer band #44546A, blue section tab #072884. Titles black #000000 bold Meiryo UI style humanist sans-serif; slide title about 3.3% of slide height, subtitle about 4.1%. Tagline white bold about 3.0%. Product names in #689BB0 bold about 3.0%, subtitles in #689BB0 bold about 2.0%. Body and bullet text dark grey #3B3838 bold about 2.2% to 2.6%. Footer message white bold about 3.3%. Copyright about 1.3% regular. Shapes have rounded corners (about 1.5% to 2% of slide height radius) and soft drop shadows on the banner, cards and boxes. Map is light grey #C8C8C8 with white background and thin white borders.

### Slide 23

**Text**

5 / Mobility Solutions Business Strategy / (2) Composite Materials / Dec. 17, 2025 / Agile exploration and rollout of new applications by leveraging diverse product lines and global sites / PP compounds / ADMER™ / Adhesive polyolefin / MILASTOMER™ / Olefin-based thermoplastic / elastomer / ARLEN™ / Polyamide 6T / AURUM™ / Thermoplastic polyimide / New composite / materials / North / America / Europe / Netherlands / Germany / China / Zhangjiagang / Japan / U.S. / Ohio / U.S. / Tennessee / Mexico / India / China / Shanghai / Thailand / South / America / Brazil / China / Zhongshan / Africa / Starting to consider / a shift to local / production / India / Pursuing a shift to / local production of / performance compounds / ASEAN / Development sites / Production sites / PP compounds / performance / compounds / Achieving greater efficiency by building setups based on local production for local / consumption worldwide and ensuring optimal operation of sites in each region / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

Full-bleed white slide with a header band across the top 8% holding a dark blue section tab with the number 5 at left, an icon, the two-line title, and the date box with the Mitsui Chemicals logo at right. Below the header a wide teal rounded panel with a centred subtitle and six white cards in one row sits above a world map that fills the middle, with blue region labels and dotted outline groupings; a legend box sits at lower right of the map. A dark slate footer band at the bottom carries the centred message, with the copyright line at its lower left. No slide number is shown beyond the tab number 5; left margin about 1.4% and right margin about 1%.

**Visuals**

Full-width world map (Mercator-style projection, Pacific-centred, Asia at centre and Americas at right) in light grey land on white sea, rendered as an image covering roughly x 2.3-99 percent and y 25-90 percent. Markers are coloured filled circles about 1.5 percent of slide width: PP compounds red #C00000, performance compounds green #70AD47, development sites yellow #FFC000. Netherlands/Germany group: red at (7.0,47.7), yellow at (7.0,50.3), green at (9.0,49.6), yellow at (9.0,52.2). Zhangjiagang group: green at (39.4,51.9), green at (41.1,54.3), red at (38.7,56.4), yellow at (38.7,59.0), yellow at (41.1,56.9). Japan group: red at (46.0,49.5), green at (46.0,52.0), yellow at (46.0,54.5). India group: red at (29.1,61.1), yellow at (29.1,63.6). Thailand/ASEAN group: red at (36.3,61.3), yellow at (36.3,64.0). Ohio group: red at (83.2,38.3), green at (83.2,40.7), yellow at (83.2,43.3). Tennessee group: red at (84.8,47.7), yellow at (84.8,50.3). Mexico: red at (80.0,55.7). Brazil group: red at (96.5,71.0), yellow at (96.5,73.6). Dotted dark slate ellipses enclose the Netherlands/Germany group, the Zhangjiagang group, the India group and an elongated ellipse around North and South America. Legend: Development sites (yellow marker), Production sites (red marker labelled PP compounds, green marker labelled performance compounds). No photographs; no charts or tables. Logos: Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right; section icon at top left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with the world map image on top of it. Clean corporate style on white. Header title and subtitle are bold black #000000 Meiryo UI-like sans-serif; title about 4.1 percent of slide height, body labels about 3 percent. Teal panel #689BB0 with white subtitle text in bold sans-serif. Cards are white with teal #689BB0 bold titles and #689CAE sub-captions. Region labels are white bold on blue #4472C4 boxes; country labels are bold grey #595959. Africa box is light green with dashed #548235 border; green caption #548235; India caption #3B3838. Footer band #44546A with white bold text. Legend box has thin dashed #44546A border, white fill. Section tab #072884 chevron. Teal panel has rounded corners and a soft grey drop shadow; cards have small rounded corners. No gradients except faint shadow under header.

### Slide 24

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Breaks a vehicle into labeled component zones with material shares and kilogram figures, making the case for plastics visible at a glance.
- Review: The density suits a circulated investor document better than a spoken presentation, and the consistent frame and the strongest pages (slides 9 and 24) keep it competent and usable, with consequential weaknesses.
- Worth keeping: Keep the repeated page frame with its numbered badge, banded takeaway headline and sector color, and the clear single-idea layouts of slide 9 (reform table beside the ROIC bubble path) and slide 24 (before-and-after battery pack with photos).

**Text**

6 / Mobility Solutions Business Strategy / (2) Composite Materials – Offering concepts / Dec. 17, 2025 / Increasing use of / plastics in motor vehicles / Creating demand by offering concepts / *Assuming a midsize, five-seater vehicle (1,300–1,400 kg) / Unit: kg/vehicle / Potential 40% quantity increase / in use of MCI products / 210–220 / 150–160 / Other / EP* / Rubber / PP / Other / EP* / Rubber / PP / Wiring / Frunk / Underbody / panels / Battery / housing / Body / system / Gasoline vehicles / EVs + improved / electric mileage / *EP: Engineering plastics / Materials / Development / Proposal / MCI products / Elastomers / Composite / materials / Procured raw materials / Concept and design / Analysis / Prototyping and assembly / Mass production / Exterior / Interior / Wiring / Vehicle battery pack / Existing item: Metal / Newly developed item: Plastic / Product concept / ・ Reduced weight / ・ Improved / recyclability / ・ Greater freedom / in design / Capturing market changes, as well as creating and / capturing new markets by offering concepts for modular components / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

White full-width slide with a dark slate header band across the top carrying the section number chevron, the title and the Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right, and a date box beneath the logo. The body is split into a left column (stacked bar chart, about 0.6 to 30 percent of width) and a right column (process flow, about 33 to 98 percent), with a row of three bottom panels (battery pack photos, existing metal item, newly developed plastic item) and a full-width dark slate banner at the bottom carrying the closing message. The page number 6 sits in a navy chevron at top left; the copyright line sits at the bottom left corner, partly under the banner edge.

**Visuals**

Stacked bar chart titled Increasing use of plastics in motor vehicles, unit kg/vehicle, with no axis scale printed. Two stacked columns: Gasoline vehicles (total printed 150–160) and EVs + improved electric mileage (total printed 210–220). Segments bottom to top in both columns: PP, Rubber, EP*, Other; no segment values are printed, so segment heights are approximate. Gasoline column about 6.1 to 11.6 percent of width, top about 30 percent, base about 52 percent of height. EV column about 21 to 26.6 percent of width, top about 21.6 percent, base about 52 percent. A thin leader line connects the 150–160 label to the top of the gasoline column and the 210–220 label to the EV column top. A light callout bubble with a pointer lists the body items Wiring, Frunk, Underbody panels, Battery housing, Body system. Process flow: a grey rounded box MCI products containing blue Elastomers and green Composite materials boxes, followed by a navy plus sign, a vertical grey box Procured raw materials, a navy multiply sign, four white vertical chevron boxes labelled Concept and design, Analysis, Prototyping and assembly, Mass production with grey arrow chevrons between them, then a navy right arrow. Right side: three rounded photo tiles labelled Exterior (vehicle exterior parts and a door mirror in colour), Interior (steering wheel and dashboard), Wiring (engine bay and wiring harness with a blue car), each semi-transparent with the label overlapping. Bottom: a photo of a silver-white midsize car in three-quarter view with a gold ellipse on the underbody battery, a dark chassis photo of an electric vehicle skateboard battery with gold ellipse, a grey metal battery tray exploded view inside a gold ellipse with green and blue layers, a dark grey plastic battery cover panel. Gold dashed lines link the car underbody ellipse to the chassis ellipse and the chassis ellipse to the metal tray ellipse. A grey right arrow sits between the metal and plastic panels.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Top band and section headers slate teal #3C6373; mid-tone header #689BB0; pale header #B0CFDC. Title text black #000000 in MeiryoUI-Bold, a bold humanist sans-serif, titles about 4.1 percent of slide height. Body labels in MeiryoUI regular sans-serif about 1.8 percent of height, bold labels about 2.6 percent. Chart text black #000000 and #3B3838. Accent blues #2F5597 and navy #0D2A6E; green #548235; light grey #D9D9D9 fills; gold #C49A00 annotation lines. Banner #44546A with white bold text and a soft drop shadow. Rectangles are square-cornered except the rounded MCI products panel and the Elastomers and Composite boxes which have small corner radii and soft shadows. Photo tiles are rounded with a white semi-transparent overlay.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Review: Many pages are very dense with small text, as on slides 4, 5, 25 and 29, and several have rough craft: the untranslated Japanese label and the ROIC row colliding with the footer on slide 3, and low-contrast white-on-pink banner headlines on slides 3 and 9.
- What would change the score: Split the densest pages (slides 4, 5, 25, 29, 30) into a lead message with one visual each, and replace the empty divider pages 15, 16, 18 and 20 and the sparse closing page 36 with an agenda that carries a takeaway.

**Text**

7 / Mobility Solutions Business Strategy / (3) Solutions & New Business / Mitsui Chemicals / Dec. 17, 2025 / Establishing new business models by deepening the solution capabilities we have / acquired and strengthened, as well as our ties with other companies / Social challenges / Mitsui Chemicals / ・Business opportunity / exploration, planning & design / ・Material development & supply / ARRK / Development / support from / development / through to small- / lot production / Internal / partners / KYOWA / ・Mold production / and supply / ・Prototyping of / components / External / partners / Examples of new businesses / Transformation of ARRK / Setting business foundations that enable continuous growth / and contribute to the Mobility Solutions Business Strategy / Structural reform / Making business operations / more efficient / Shifting to asset-light structure / Pursuing a / growth strategy / Extending business model to / encompass offering solutions / Materials / Design / Mass / production / Other functions / Finished / product / New / transport / system / Glydways / Suzuki / Motor / Corporation / ARRK / Vehicle for new / transport system / Innovative / materials / develop- / ment / Mitsui Chemicals / PP compounds / for 3D printers / Auto parts / Toyota Motor / Corporation / concept car / Robot / develop- / ment / ARRK / SoftBank Robotics / Corporation / With social challenges as our starting point, we are pursuing / new business development that taps into solution capabilities / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

A seven-row-by-four-column style grid: a title block across the top with the section number 7 in a navy chevron tab at the far left, the title at top left and the date box and Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right, a full-width blue subtitle band beneath it, and a dark footer band at the bottom. The left half holds a boxed diagram (Social challenges, Mitsui Chemicals, ARRK, KYOWA, External partners) above a Transformation of ARRK section with two rounded callouts; a large grey chevron arrow labelled Examples of new businesses separates it from the right half, a matrix with four column chevrons (Materials, Design, Mass production / Other functions, Finished product) and three row labels (New transport system, Innovative materials development, Robot development). Title and footer text are left and centre aligned; copyright text sits at the bottom left and there is no slide number other than the 7 in the header tab.

**Visuals**

Diagram and matrix with photographs and logos, no charts. Left diagram: a light blue ellipse (#ABC9D5 at 50 percent opacity) sits behind three white rounded boxes, Mitsui Chemicals (top, centre) listing business opportunity exploration, planning and design and material development and supply; ARRK (bottom left) with its blue logo and text Development support from development through to small-lot production; and KYOWA (bottom right, red outlined logo) listing mold production and supply and prototyping of components. A grey down-up double arrow (#BFBFBF) joins Internal partners to External partners (cream box #F6EACE). Above the ellipse, a grey downward arrow leads from the Social challenges label. Lower left: two white rounded callout boxes with thick blue-grey borders (#689BB0) labelled Structural reform (making business operations more efficient; shifting to asset-light structure) and Pursuing a growth strategy (extending business model to encompass offering solutions), each with an underlined title, and a small light-blue upward chevron between them. Right matrix: thin blue-grey grid lines (#689BB0) divide three rows and four columns. Row New transport system: Mitsui Chemicals logo in the Materials column; cream boxes Glydways (Design) and Suzuki Motor Corporation (Mass production); ARRK logo under them; top-right photo of a white autonomous shuttle pod with a wide black glass band and dark grey wheels, shown full colour on white, captioned Vehicle for new transport system. Row Innovative materials development: Mitsui Chemicals logo; a small round photo of a grey-white cylindrical container with a light interior in the Materials column captioned PP compounds for 3D printers; ARRK logo above a photo of four glossy black plastic parts on white in the Design column; right photo of a silver-white SUV concept car on a black background, three-quarter front view, lit from above, boxed, captioned Auto parts and Toyota Motor Corporation concept car. Row Robot development: Mitsui Chemicals logo in the Materials column; ARRK logo and cream box SoftBank Robotics Corporation in the Design column; right tall photo of a white and grey service robot with a display screen on top, standing against a dark blue wall, boxed. Top-right Mitsui Chemicals logo: blue swoosh with grey wordmark. Header icon: a light blue-grey square tile with a dark line icon of a seated person and sound waves.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 26

**Text**

0→1 MAKE IT HAPPEN
Business Strategy Presentation
ICT Solutions
Mitsui Chemicals
平原 彰男
HIRAHARA Akio
Representative Director,
Senior Managing Executive Officer,
Business Sector President,
ICT Solutions Business Sector
Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.
December 17, 2025

**Layout**

Title slide with a white field. The 0→1 MAKE IT HAPPEN logo sits at top left, with the title block left-aligned at about 11% from the left edge, roughly centred vertically. The presenter block sits at bottom left, with a thin vertical divider separating the Mitsui Chemicals logo from the name and title lines. A large blue chevron fills the right 42% of the slide, and the date sits bottom right on it. The copyright line sits at the bottom left margin with no slide number.

**Visuals**

Three non-text visuals. (1) A full-height blue chevron illustration on the right, about 42% of slide width, spanning the full slide height. Its left edge is a diagonal notch: the white field cuts into it from top-left to a point at about 90% of width, mid-height, then returns to bottom-left, so the blue forms a right-pointing arrowhead. Solid blue #00449A, flat, no gradient, no shadow. (2) The 0→1 MAKE IT HAPPEN logo at top left: the word MAKE IT HAPPEN in condensed bold sans caps in grey #6D6E71, with the '0' and '1' in grey and a right-pointing arrow between them in light blue #1A9AD6. (3) The Mitsui Chemicals logo: a stylised swoosh of three curved bands in blue gradient from dark #0B4FA0 to light #1AA3E0, beside the grey #5A5A5A wordmark 'Mitsui Chemicals' in a bold sans serif. No charts, tables or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF field with a solid blue #00449A chevron shape on the right.
