**Layout**

Full-width white header band across the top with a dark blue section tab holding the number 1 at left, a gold icon square, the bold title ICT Solutions Business Strategy left-aligned near the top, the Mitsui Chemicals logo and a grey date box at top right. Below, a cream band carries the left-aligned headline block; beneath it a bar chart occupies the left column (about 0 to 20% width) and a large rounded Semiconductor panel spans the upper right, with three rounded panels (Imaging, Converting, Battery materials) in a row along the bottom right. Copyright line sits bottom-left at about 1% margin; no slide number other than the section tab.

**Visuals**

Bar chart with a rising curved gold arrow. Chart type: vertical bar chart with four bars, each with a gradient fill from gold #D8A73A-type at the base to pale cream at the top and a dark brown smudge near the middle. Bars labelled from left: FY24 Result 26.7B yen with ROIC 7%; FY25 Outlook 35.5B yen with ROIC 8%; FY28 Target 61B yen with ROIC 11%; FY30 Target 73B yen with ROIC 13%. Bar heights are proportional to the printed values (about 26.7, 35.5, 61, 73 billion yen); no axis scale or units axis is printed beyond the labels. The baseline is a thin grey horizontal axis line spanning the chart width. A thick curved arrow with a gold-to-brown gradient rises from just above the FY24 bar to a head pointing up-right above the FY28 bar. Diffrar logo: a blue rounded square mark with a white D and the wordmark Diffrar in spaced dark letters. Diffrar photo: a dark near-black background with pale translucent circular glass-like discs and a violet-lit thin film strip lying diagonally, lit from the side, boxed with square corners. Chip photo: a blue and purple illustrated microchip on a dark circuit board with glowing pink light, boxed. Battery photo: a stack of flat dark grey lithium-ion pouch cells with gold and silver tabs on a light grey background, boxed, cropped square. Mitsui Chemicals logo: blue swoosh mark with dark grey wordmark at top right. Gold icon at top: a white outlined network/document icon on gold square.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Title text black #000000 in bold Meiryo UI, sans-serif, about 4.1% of slide height (about 3.3% for the section tab number). Headline block: black #000000 and red #C00000 bold Meiryo UI sans-serif, about 3.7% height, with body-level lines about 2.2 to 2.6% height. Japanese subhead colour teal #3C6474. Accent colours: gold #BF9000 for chart values, dark gold #88661A for the Semiconductor label panel, gold #D8A73A for Imaging label panel, light gold #E7C985 for Converting, pale cream #F6EACE for Battery label. Battery label text grey #A6A6A6. Cream band colour about #F8EDD5 (white overlay at 80% over #D8A73A). Section tab #072884 with white number. Date box dark grey #595959 with white bold text. Panels have rounded outlines in dark gold #88661A (large panel) and gold #D8A73A (bottom panels) with rounded corner radii about 2 to 3% of width; label tabs are solid rounded rectangles with vertical white text. Body copy in bold black Meiryo UI with bullets as small black dots in Arial. Chart values in bold black Meiryo UI. A subtle grey drop shadow under header band. No gradient background.

### Slide 28

**Text**

2 / ICT Solutions Business: Key Investment Projects, / Plus Business Restructuring & Optimization / Mitsui Chemicals / Dec. 17, 2025 / Speeding up growth of key businesses through active investment in / priority sectors while also pursuing business restructuring & optimization / Semiconductor & assembly / Imaging / Converting / Sectors / Project / Timing / Capacity / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY23 / FY24 / FY25 / FY26 Onward / Key investment projects / Semiconductor & assembly / ICROS Tape / Launch of ICROS Tape operations in Taiwan / January 2020 / 3.8million m2 / Launch of operations by Mitsui Chemicals ICT Materia / April 2024 / - / Increase in ICROS Tape capacity in Taiwan / June 2024 / 3.8million m2 / MITSUI PELLICLE / New EUV pellicle facility / April 2021 / - / Acquisition of Asahi Kasei pellicle business / July 2023 / - / Investment in CNT pellicle production facility / March 2026 / 5,000 sheets / Next-gen / Investment in SHINKO ELECTRIC INDUSTRIES / March 2025 / - / Others / Tender offer for Honshu Chemical Industry / June 2021 / - / Incorporation of Nippon Aluminum Alkyls as a wholly owned subsidiary / October 2025 / - / Imaging / Investment in new APEL plant / June 2022 / +50% / Converting / Acquisition of DIC Kako by Japan Composite / December 2020 / +70% / April 2023: Absorption-type merger by Japan Composite / Increase in polyurethane dispersion capacity / June 2025 / +100% / Increase in XDI special isocyanate capacity / January 2026 / +20% / Restructuring & optimization / Split and partial transfer of shares in Mitsui Chemicals Tohcello / April 2024 / Transfer of shares in Katsuzai Chemical / July 2024 / Transfer of shares in DM Novafoam / March 2025 / Withdrawal from toner binder resin business / June 2025 / Withdrawal from nitrogen trifluoride business / During 2026 / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a Gantt-style table. A blue section tab with the number 2 sits at the top-left, followed by a gold icon box and a two-line bold title left-aligned at about x 11 and y 1 to 8. The Mitsui Chemicals logo sits top-right, with a grey date box below it. A gold banner spans the width under the title at y 9 to 16. A three-row legend sits at the top-right corner. The main table runs from x 3 to 100 and y 17 to 96, with a left vertical yellow band reading Key investment projects. A second table for restructuring sits below at y 77 to 100, with a grey vertical label on the left. A small copyright line sits bottom-left at y 98.8.

**Visuals**

Gantt-style project table with a timeline across columns FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, FY24, FY25 and FY26 Onward. Dark brown bars (#88661A) mark the Semiconductor and assembly projects, gold bars (#D8A73A) mark the Imaging project, and light tan bars (#E7C985) mark the Converting projects. Bars run from their start year to the right edge, which is FY26 Onward. Row 1, Launch of ICROS Tape operations in Taiwan, starts at the FY20 left edge. Row 2, Launch of operations by Mitsui Chemicals ICT Materia, starts in about mid FY24. Row 3, Increase in ICROS Tape capacity in Taiwan, starts in about late FY24. Row 4, New EUV pellicle facility, starts in about early FY21. Row 5, Acquisition of Asahi Kasei pellicle business, starts in about early FY23. Row 6, Investment in CNT pellicle production facility, starts in about FY26 Onward. Row 7, Investment in SHINKO ELECTRIC INDUSTRIES, starts in about early FY25. Row 8, Tender offer for Honshu Chemical Industry, starts in about mid FY21. Row 9, Incorporation of Nippon Aluminum Alkyls, starts in about mid FY25. Row 10, Investment in new APEL plant, starts in about early FY22 and is gold. Row 11, Acquisition of DIC Kako by Japan Composite, is a light tan bar from about FY20 to the right edge, with the label April 2023: Absorption-type merger by Japan Composite printed inside it. Row 12, Increase in polyurethane dispersion capacity, starts in about FY25. Row 13, Increase in XDI special isocyanate capacity, starts in about late FY25. The restructuring table has five downward-pointing grey triangles (#808080). They mark April 2024 near FY24, July 2024 just right of it, March 2025 near FY25, June 2025 near the FY25 right side, and During 2026 at the FY26 Onward section. Thin diagonal lines in the empty cells of the Sectors and Capacity columns run from lower-left to upper-right. The Mitsui Chemicals logo is a blue swoosh with a grey wordmark, top-right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title is bold black Meiryo UI, about 3.7 percent of slide height. Banner text is white bold Meiryo UI, about 3.0 percent. Table text is regular black Meiryo UI, about 1.8 percent, and header labels are bold black about 1.85 percent. Sector labels are bold white on brown. Accents are gold #D8A73A, brown #88661A, and light tan #E7C985. Table borders are thin black lines about 0.1 percent wide. Bars have square corners and no shadow. The left band is pale yellow #FFE699 and the restructuring label is light grey #D9D9D9. The top-left tab is navy #072884 with a chevron edge. The date box is dark grey.

### Slide 29

**Review notes**

- Review: Many pages are very dense with small text, as on slides 4, 5, 25 and 29, and several have rough craft: the untranslated Japanese label and the ROIC row colliding with the footer on slide 3, and low-contrast white-on-pink banner headlines on slides 3 and 9.
- What would change the score: Split the densest pages (slides 4, 5, 25, 29, 30) into a lead message with one visual each, and replace the empty divider pages 15, 16, 18 and 20 and the sparse closing page 36 with an agenda that carries a takeaway.

**Layout**

Full-width 16:9 slide with a white header strip containing a navy section tab numbered 3 at left, a gold icon tile, the bold black title at top left and the Mitsui Chemicals logo and grey date box at top right. A full-width gold banner sits below the header with a two-line centred headline. The body is a flow diagram: a horizontal Front-end process chain (top-left) and Back-end/packaging chain (top-right) in beige panels, with a Substrate chain (lower-left) and an Assembly panel (lower-right) and a central red-bordered text box; the copyright line sits bottom left. Margins are about 1 percent; no slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Process-flow diagram with product photographs and logos. Front-end chain (left to right): Silicon wafer circle, then gold chevron arrow band with Film formation, Exposure, Etching, CMP, Cleaning, Testing, then Semiconductor wafer circle. Back-end chain: Back-grinding, Dicing, IC pickup (small photo of wafer on blue tape), Die bonding, Molding, Testing, then Semiconductor package circle. Substrate chain: Resin substrate circle, then Drilling, Plating, Exposure, Etching, Stack-up, then Package substrate circle. Product cards are rounded rectangles with gold (#8A6A1E) outlines and light grey (#F2F2F2) fill, each with a small product photo: Disilane gas cylinders, Trimethylaluminum cylinders, MITSUI PELLICLE membrane photo, MILEX Phenols, FORTIMO TAKENATE pad with grey polishing pad, Anthraquinone bottle, PIVAR vial, ICROS Tape rolls, Bonding materials 3D-package wafer, Paltref film roll, Transparent adhesive wafer disc, SYNTEX Nano filter cartridges (two), Gigafreq cabinet, MITSUI PELLICLE frame, Opulent release film, Mitsui Masking Tape rolls, Capacitor chip, SP-PET film roll and Module board circle. Red arrows from callout boxes point to cards; gold connector lines link circles to cards. Arrows: a thick grey-gold arrow runs from the Substrate package circle up and right to the Back-end chain and a thick grey arrow from Capacitor to SP-PET then down to Module. Legend box in bottom-right of centre with colour key.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: Split the densest pages (slides 4, 5, 25, 29, 30) into a lead message with one visual each, and replace the empty divider pages 15, 16, 18 and 20 and the sparse closing page 36 with an agenda that carries a takeaway.

**Layout**

Full-width header band with a dark blue numbered tab '4' at top left, a gold icon tile, the title in bold black at top, the Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right and the date box beneath it. A gold full-width banner sits below the header. The main area is split into a left illustration zone (process images, bar chart, labels), a centre text box in the lower middle, and a right-hand map of Japan and Asia with site callouts and a legend; a yellow note box sits bottom right. The copyright line sits bottom left, with no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

1) Stacked bar chart, top left, titled by the CAGR box. Categories on the x-axis: 24, 25, 28, 30 (fiscal years). Series: Back-grinding (gold #D8A73A, bottom) and Peripheral domains (dark teal #156082, top). Values are not printed; about: 24 total about 1.0 units, 25 about 1.3, 28 about 2.0, 30 about 3.0 with the teal top segment about 25% of the bar in 30. Pale gold up-arrow (#E7C985) runs behind the bars. Callout box lists CAGR 24–28: ICROS Tape 10% (red #C00000), in peripheral domains 30% (red). 2) Process illustration: a wafer on a dicing frame (grey ring with blue-tinted wafer) in the Dicing zone, a back-grinding wafer on a grey chuck, a wafer with a dicing blade, a stack of thin chip tape with a down arrow, a microscope photo of a dicing surface, a molded package on a pink-tinted ellipse, a red and black thermal release tape roll and disc, and a stack of wafers on a blue ellipse; orange and gold arrows connect the stages. 3) Japan and Southeast Asia outline map in grey line art on white, with pale blue circles marking regions and blue (existing sales) and green (production) location pins; blue lines connect CIL (Nagoya) and the callout to pins. 4) Photo of CIL (Nagoya) building, dark grey facility with ATTA sign, framed in a blue box. 5) Photo of Nagoya Works mother plant, large white-grey factory building under blue sky, framed in green. 6) Photo of Taiwan plant, multi-storey grey factory, framed in green. 7) Legend icons: blue pin, green pin, pale blue circle.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a large pale grey circle (#F2F2F2) behind the process illustration. White background with a gold banner (#D8A73A) across the slide top section. Titles are bold black (#000000) sans-serif (Meiryo UI Bold style, a Gothic sans) at about 4.1% of slide height for the main title and about 3.3% for the banner. Body text is bold black sans-serif about 2.0% of slide height. Accent colours: dark blue #003A8C tab, blue #0000FF labels and boxes, gold-brown #7F6000 and #88661A for headings and outlines, red #C00000 for emphasis, green #008000 for Taiwan callouts, teal #156082 for peripheral domains. Boxes have hard square corners, thin borders of 0.75 to 2 pt, and no shadows. Pale fills: #F8CBAD peach, #FFF2CC cream, #DCE6F2 pale blue. Blue label blocks use white bold text.

**Layout**

Full-width header with the navy number tab '5' at the far left, the two-line title beside a gold square icon, the Mitsui Chemicals logo top-right and a grey date box below it; a full-width gold banner spans y 10-18%. Below, two columns separated by a thin vertical black rule at about x 51%: the left column holds the EUV pellicle bar chart, the requirement line, the boxed process circles and three bullets, and the right column holds the lithography roadmap, the Logic and Memory commentary. The copyright line sits bottom-left at y 98.8%, with no slide number apart from the tab numeral; margins are about 1%.

**Visuals**

1) Bar chart with no printed bar values except CAGR: five vertical bars for 24, 25, 26, 27, 28 on a shared baseline at about y 52%; bars 24 and 25 are plain light blue #8EA9DB at about 3-4 units tall, bar 26 is about 6 units with a navy-to-light gradient top, bar 27 about 9 units and bar 28 about 12 units, both with a navy #1F3864 top fading to #8EA9DB at the base, values about (no printed values). Axes: x-axis labels 24 25 26 27 28 in grey #595959; no y-axis or units printed. A grey #E6E6E6 band behind the 28 region labelled Outlook in grey #A6A6A6 marks the forecast years. CNT label in red #C00000 above bar 26 with a gold #D8A73A down arrow; Next-gen CNT label in red above bar 27 with a gold down arrow. A rising light gold #E7C985 arrow runs from about x6 y52 to x41 y36 across the bars. Callout box with a dark gold #8B6914 outline and rounded corners reading CAGR 24-28 (Volume) 30% with 30% in red. 2) Gold downward triangle #D8A73A under the Higher output text at about x52-99 y52-55. 3) Lithography roadmap: Logic chevron row in navy outlines and Memory row in blue #0070C0 label boxes; technology chevrons 3 nm, 2 nm, A14, A10 (Logic) and 1B, 1C, 1D, 0A, 0B, 0C (Memory) with white fills and blue outlines. Lithography systems purple #7030A0 block with Low-NA and High-NA tabs. Bars: NXE3800E #4D35FB (500 W), NXE3800F #3398FD with red #C00000 600 W badge, NXE4200G #01CDFD, NXE4250H teal #00CCBB; EXE5000 #4D35FB (350 W), EX5200B #4D35FB with red 600 W badge, EXE5200C #3398FD, EXE5400D #01CDFD, EXE5450E teal #00CCBB; all chevron-pointed arrows. Year axis 2024 to 2031 Onward in bold black with a horizontal black rule above and below. Red vertical lines with red arrowheads run from the year axis down to the labels Next-gen CNT pellicles and CNT pellicles. 4) Gold downward triangle #D8A73A below the Low-NA and High-NA notes at about x57-91 y70-73. 5) Process diagram in a black-outlined rectangle at about x4.4-50.2 y64-85: navy #152589 circles labelled >88% FY21, >90% FY22, >92% FY25, >94% FY27 with opacity 0.59 and 0.50 on the last two; light blue #4FC3F7 chevron arrows between circles; a light blue striped arrow pointing to 1,000-watt-compatible; black double-headed arrow under Conventional materials; gold #D8A73A solid arrow labelled CNT and dashed gold arrow labelled Beyond-next-gen pointing right with Next-gen between. 6) Mitsui Chemicals logo: blue-grey swoosh with dark grey wordmark. 7) Icon: white line-art node diagram on a gold #D8A73A square at the title start. 8) Vertical black divider line and horizontal black rules as separators.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

**Layout**

The header runs across the top with a navy section tab showing 6 at left, a gold icon tile, the two-line bold title at top left and the Mitsui Chemicals logo with a grey date box at top right, above a full-width gold subtitle band; the body is a left-to-right flow of a tan panel, a grey SHINKO panel and a grey right column holding the CIL panel, with six product cards in a two-column grid at far right under a heading, a dashed impact box across the bottom, and a copyright line at bottom left with no slide number other than the section tab.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled Projected shipments of next-gen semiconductor packages (units) with three blue #00AEEF bars for 2021, 2025 and 2028 on a light grey baseline; no axis values, units range or legend are printed, so the bars are about 30 percent, about 60 percent and about 100 percent of the 2028 height respectively, with no highlighted points. A tan #E7C985 block arrow rises from above the 2021 bar to the upper right, partly overlapping the top of the 2025 bar. A white CAGR box with a brown border reads CAGR FY21–28 15% with 15 in bold red. Flow diagram: grey arrows link Various materials and their molecular design to Evaluation of basic characteristics and link Process compatibility evaluation to Prototyping and then to the right column; a brown vertical connector runs from Process compatibility evaluation down to the Evaluating the entire package substrate production process box; a blue circular double-arc loop labelled Combine technologies sits between the left and middle panels; a dashed blue line runs down from Issues in the production process to the impact box. Inside the middle loop, Process A, Process F and Process G are tall rounded boxes joined by gold arrows and a gold return loop. Left panel: four small boxes labelled Process A, Process C, Process E and Process G inside the Evaluating each individual process box. Right column: chip diagram with two dark grey #595959 dies labelled Logic and Memory, a green Optical IC block with white up arrow, a light blue Interposer, a light green Semiconductor Package substrate and grey solder balls, with grey optical block and blue and yellow waveguide lines at right. Photograph of the CIL building: exterior of a low-rise dark blue and white industrial building with ATTA signage, palm trees at left and blue daytime sky, boxed rectangle with no border, cropped at its edges. Logos: Mitsui Chemicals swoosh logo at top right and inside the left panel, and SHINKO logo on white in the middle panel. Product photos: ICROS Tape photo of a pink tape roll beside a dark grey disc on teal; MITSUI PELLICLE photo of a grey metal frame with mesh; Bonding materials photo of a dark grey bonding chuck on white; Temporary bonding material photo of a clear vial and a stacked disc diagram with Support substrate, Temporary bonding material and Wafer layers in pink, orange and blue; Transparent adhesive photo of a dark object above a white dish on white. Optical waveguide material has no photo. Gold right-pointing chevron arrow at right of the cards.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with a navy #072884 section tab and a gold #D8A73A full-width subtitle band; titles in black #000000 and body text in near-black #242424; accents in red #C00000, green #008000, cyan #00AEEF and brown-gold #8B6914 borders; the typeface is Meiryo UI Bold for headings and labels, a humanist sans-serif, with Meiryo UI regular for captions; title about 3.7 percent of slide height, headline about 3.0 percent, body labels about 1.5 to 2.2 percent. Panels have soft rounded corners with flat fills and no shadows; product cards have rounded brown-gold borders on light grey #F2F2F2; the impact box has a dashed cyan border; the subtitle text has a slight dark drop shadow.

**Layout**

A full-width header band holds a navy chevron with the slide number 7 at left, a gold icon tile, the title Imaging Solutions : Diffrar™ and the Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right with a grey date box beneath it. A full-width gold banner below carries the centred two-line subtitle. The body is two columns: left column (about x 1 to 44) has a grey heading panel, the AR glasses photo and three bullets with a footnote; right column (about x 47 to 99) has a grey heading panel, collaboration text, a photo of glasses on a stand, a Media-screen photo pair with a green arrow, a waveguide cross-section diagram with labels, and a closing text block. Left margin is about 1 percent, and a copyright line sits at bottom left.

**Visuals**

Photograph 1 (left column, x 1.8 to 41, y 32.6 to 78.7, boxed, not cropped): three thin clear circular glass wafers of decreasing size stacked diagonally on a black background, with a pair of rimless glasses lying in front on a lavender-grey cloth; lighting is dark and low-key with violet-lilac highlights on the wafer edges and cloth; dominant colours black, lilac and grey. Photograph 2 (right, x 49.6 to 65.2, y 47.7 to 66.3, boxed): black AR glasses with thick frames on a black stand and cable, in front of a white brick wall, on a wooden table; a blue dashed ellipse outlines the lenses and two blue dashed lines run from the ellipse to the Media screen photo. Photograph 3 (x 43.7 to 58.3, y 67.2 to 86.4, boxed): a dark display screen menu with faint menu items on the left, a clock reading 7:58 PM with date 2020.10.17, a calendar icon showing 16 and a weather icon reading 23.5 °C; the word Media in green is circled with a white ellipse outline. Green block arrow (x 58.3 to 60.4, y 71 to 83) points from photo 3 to photo 4. Photograph 4 (x 60.7 to 76.5, y 67 to 86, boxed): a display showing the word Media in green at top and a colourful rainbow-coloured video thumbnail with a white circular play button and a small heart SHARE icon beneath. Diagram (right): a light blue (#B4C7E7) horizontal slab representing the polymer substrate with two small grey rectangular couplers on top at its left and right ends, diagonal ray lines zigzagging inside it, vertical blue arrows down from the couplers to the eye icons, a grey ellipse shadow beneath; a dark blue (#0070C0) box labelled Optical waveguide sits below the right end, a blue leader line runs from the label Polymer substrate for optical waveguide down to the slab; a speech-bubble icon and two grey eye icons sit at lower right. Logos: Mitsui Chemicals logo (blue swoosh mark with grey wordmark) at top right. Icon: white line-art icon of two overlapping rounded rectangles on a gold tile at top left. Small hand-pointer icon in blue before When the user selects the content.

### Slide 34

**Text**

8 / Converting Solutions: / Global Expansion of Coating & Engineering Materials / Mitsui Chemicals / Dec. 17, 2025 / Speeding up business expansion by rolling out / products tailored to environmental needs to core markets / • Expanding sales regions / • Establishing production sites and increasing capacity / in response to global demand / CAGR 24–30(volume) / Total for coatings & / engineering materials 6% / POD 9% / PUD 15% / FY24 / FY25 / FY28 FY30 / Europe / Cultivating markets with / an eye to shifting to / local mass production / China / Japan / Increasing / production / capacity / U.S. / Considering / capacity boosts / and creation of / technical / support setups / Middle / East / Thailand / Africa / India / Considering creation / of sales and technical / support setups / Increasing / production / capacity / Malaysia / Increasing / production / capacity / South / America / Bolstering our / sales setup / Current sales / regions / Regions to be / newly cultivated / Overseas sites / India / Coating tech center (CTC) / Bolstering & / enhancing / capabilities / Middle / East / Africa / Using the CTC in India to provide / technical support and cultivate markets / • Technical support / • Medium-scale prototyping and / sample production / • Development of brands suited to local needs / ⇒ Considering creation of sales and technical support / setups to tap into local needs / Copyright © 2025 Mitsui Chemicals, Inc.

**Layout**

Full-width header band at top with navy slide-number tab at far left, gold-outlined icon, two-line bold title left-aligned at x 11 percent, Mitsui Chemicals logo and grey date box at top right; gold full-width banner beneath the header carries a centred two-line subtitle. Below the banner, a bar chart with CAGR box sits at left, a boxed three-line bullet statement sits top centre over a world map that fills the middle and right, and a wide peach panel spans the bottom with the India CTC content and a copyright line at the bottom left.

**Visuals**

Chart: a simple column chart with four gold bars labelled on the x-axis FY24, FY25, FY28 and FY30, with no values printed on the bars; estimated heights about 60 percent, 62 percent, 75 percent and 85 percent of the chart height, rising left to right. A pale gold arrow (#E7C985, about 50 percent opacity) rises diagonally above the bars from lower left to upper right. The CAGR box in the chart shows Total for coatings and engineering materials 6 percent, POD 9 percent and PUD 15 percent, with the 9 percent and 15 percent figures in red. There is no y-axis title or scale. Map: a grey outline world map drawn in thin grey lines on white, with translucent gold circles (current sales regions) over Europe, China, India, Japan (approx), Malaysia and the U.S., and translucent blue circles (regions to be newly cultivated) over Africa, the Middle East and South America. Blue outlined map pins with blue dots mark overseas sites in India, Thailand and Malaysia and one pin each near China and the U.S.; a green pin with green dot marks the site near Japan. Two curved arrows point from the India pin: a short dark red-brown arrow to the Middle East and a longer gold-to-red gradient arrow down-left to Africa. Legend box at lower right with three entries: a light gold circle labelled Current sales regions, a light blue circle labelled Regions to be newly cultivated, and a blue outlined pin labelled Overseas sites. Bottom panel: a photograph of an industrial coating machine (large grey and olive-brown frame with rollers, photographed straight-on, rectangular, boxed without border, neutral indoor lighting). A green-outlined speech-bubble callout reading Bolstering and enhancing capabilities points to the bullet list. A large right-pointing block arrow with gradient from olive #88661A to dark red #C00000 and a white outline links the CTC box to the Middle East and Africa labels.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title text black (#000000) MeiryoUI-Bold, two lines, about 3.7 percent of slide height. Subtitle text white (#FFFFFF) bold with a soft dark drop shadow on a gold band (#D8A73A). Body text black (#000000) bold; red accent text #C00000; green accent #008000; grey text #404040 and #595959. Region labels are white bold text on olive-brown boxes #88661A. Navy tab #072884 with white slide number. Chart bars and banner in gold #D8A73A. Pale gold #FFD966 at 50 percent opacity for current regions; pale blue #9DC3E6 at 50 percent opacity for new regions. Typefaces: MeiryoUI-Bold (humanist sans-serif, bold) for nearly all text, Aptos Narrow (sans-serif) for FY axis labels, ArialMT for bullet glyphs. Title about 3.7 percent of slide height; body about 2.2 percent; footer about 1.3 percent. Shapes have square corners except the rounded-edged CTC box and legend box, with thin olive borders (#88661A, about 1.5 pt) on the bullet box, CAGR box, legend and CTC box. The bottom panel has a thin dark slate border (#44546A) and no shadow. Gradients appear only on the large block arrow and the curved arrow.

**Layout**

Standard widescreen slide with a navy numbered tab and title across the top, a Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right and a date box beneath it. A full-width gold banner carries three white headline lines below the header. Below that, three columns run left to right: a key-features outlined box with a gold arrow, a stack of four candidate boxes beside a vertical gradient arrow, and a right-hand zone with example products, a red statement, a gold chevron and a dotted-border panel holding two product cards. Copyright line sits bottom left and the 'High heat resistance' label sits bottom centre-right; there is no slide-number footer beyond the navy tab.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a brown coated roller with white end caps on a green machine (Paltref film roll), in a boxed cream panel at about x 53 y 65.5 w 12.3 h 14.5; the roller is diagonal, centred, with a yellow-green lower frame. Photograph of paper cups of french fries and fried chicken pieces on a white background (BONRON food packaging), boxed at about x 73.4 y 65.5 w 12.3 h 14.5, neatly framed, bright even lighting. Photograph of a burger and kraft paper wrapped sandwiches printed with a pattern on a grey background (BONRON packaging), boxed at about x 86 y 65.6 w 11.9 h 14.1, shadowed, neutral lighting. Vertical gradient arrow from light blue #DEEBF7 at top through pale peach to #F8CBAD at the arrowhead, pointing down beside the candidate boxes, labelled High heat resistance. Gold right-pointing chevron between the feature box and candidate column. Mitsui Chemicals logo: blue swoosh and grey wordmark, top right. Gold rounded-square icon with a white line-art diagram at top left, next to the navy tab.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title and key lines in black MeiryoUI-Bold style sans-serif, title about 3.7% of slide height, body about 2.6 to 3.0%. Gold accent #D8A73A for the headline banner and header boxes, with white bold text on them. Red #C00000 for emphasis statements and the panel heading; blue #0000FF for product names Paltref and BONRON; black #000000 for body text; grey #595959 for copyright. Navy #072884 numbered tab; navy #1F3864 outline on the feature box. Candidate boxes use light blue #DEEBF7 and peach #F8CBAD with rounded corners of about 15 px. Grey #E7E6E6 boxes for property claims. Cream #F6EACE panels with a gold dotted border. Headline banner has a subtle drop shadow on its white text. No gradient apart from the vertical arrow.
